## 002. Aegis of Celestial Fire

Handle: `he434f1d7g953dg1b5bgb8a0gf6d8bb375a64`.

| Language | Previous content | Updated content | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| English reference | — | A silver shield bearing a shard of Mount Celestia. It purged the undead with sacred flame. Its last bearer fell to a lich, but the Aegis burned for three days, and the whispers of freed souls still echo within it. | — |
| Russian | Щит весит вдвое меньше, чем должен — 12 фунтов вместо 25. Золотой крест не нанесен, а проступил сам после сотни изгнаний нежити в Баровии. Паладин Гавриил, специалист по упокоению, оставил на внутренней стороне список из 1300 имен — души, которые он освободил. Ихор нежити испаряется с поверхности со звуком, похожим на молитву. | Серебряный щит, несущий осколок горы Селестия. Он истреблял нежить священным пламенем. Его последний владелец пал от руки лича, но Эгида горела ещё три дня, и шёпот освобождённых душ до сих пор звучит внутри неё. | Russian retained obsolete unrelated flavour (12/25 pounds, 1300 names); replace with current official English story, retaining its three-day duration. |

<a id="entry-005"></a>

## 005. Amulet of Armour Resonance

Handle: `h83887fb6g59f1gf206g3864gcc3f7504cd8c`.

| Language | Previous content | Updated content | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| English reference | — | Dwarf-wrought, it sings a harmony between plate and shield, turning gear into a living fortress. Found amidst shattered weapons in a fallen hall, its runes yet glow for a warrior who understands strength in unity. | — |
| Russian | Дварфийская работа: рубин огранен в форме молота, золотая оправа имитирует наковальню. Король Торек Железнобородый носил его 400 лет, не снимая. Камень синхронизирует звенья кольчуги — они двигаются как единый организм, а не отдельные куски металла. На обороте выцарапано: "Падение Подгорья, день 1287-й осады. Мы не сдались." | Созданный дварфами, этот амулет связывает доспех и щит в гармоничное целое, превращая снаряжение в живую крепость. Найденный среди разбитого оружия в разрушенном зале, он по-прежнему светится рунами, ожидая воина, который понимает силу единства. | Russian retained different obsolete flavour with unsupported 400 years and siege day 1287; translate the current official English flavour instead. |

| English reference | — |  [Legit at this mode's default]<br><br>A powerful enemy above half its hit points that takes no damage on its own turn gains an Action Surge the next turn. | — |
| French |  [Légitime tant que cela correspond au réglage par défaut de ce mode]<br><br>Un ennemi puissant ayant plus de la moitié de ses points de vie et n'ayant subi aucun dégât pendant son propre tour gagne une action supplémentaire au tour suivant. |  [Légitime tant que cela correspond au réglage par défaut de ce mode]<br><br>Un ennemi puissant ayant plus de la moitié de ses points de vie et n'ayant subi aucun dégât pendant son propre tour gagne Fougue au tour suivant. | Restore the actual Action Surge status name and tooltip, rather than an unnamed extra action. |
| German |  [Legitim, solange es dem Modus-Standard entspricht]<br><br>Ein mächtiger Gegner mit mehr als der Hälfte seiner Trefferpunkte, der in seinem eigenen Zug keinen Schaden erlitten hat, erhält im nächsten Zug eine zusätzliche Aktion. |  [Legitim, solange es dem Modus-Standard entspricht]<br><br>Ein mächtiger Gegner mit mehr als der Hälfte seiner Trefferpunkte, der in seinem eigenen Zug keinen Schaden erlitten hat, erhält im nächsten Zug Tatendrang. | Restore the actual Action Surge status name and tooltip, rather than an unnamed extra action. |
| Italian |  [Legittimo finché corrisponde al valore predefinito della modalità]<br><br>Un nemico potente con più della metà dei suoi punti ferita che non ha subito danni durante il proprio turno ottiene un'azione extra nel turno successivo. |  [Legittimo finché corrisponde al valore predefinito della modalità]<br><br>Un nemico potente con più della metà dei suoi punti ferita che non ha subito danni durante il proprio turno ottiene Impeto d’Azione nel turno successivo. | Restore the actual Action Surge status name and tooltip, rather than an unnamed extra action. |

## 132. Northern Bow

Handle: `hf1a54381gafeag4223g8133gf9ef28492fb5`.

| Language | Previous content | Updated content | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| English reference | — | It is no secret that the snowy lands are swarming with all sorts of monsters of unimaginable size, and the northern peoples had to come up with all sorts of inventions to survive in such an unfavourable environment. One such invention was the northern bow, which was believed to be a gift to the Quiet Tundra Clan after numerous sacrifices to the northern idols. This bow was incredibly powerful and could strike particularly large beasts and monsters that even a group of hunters would never have been able to handle. | — |
| Simplified Chinese | 众所周知，冰天雪地的极北之地到处都是体型超乎想象的怪物。为了在这般恶劣的环境中生存，北方民族不得不穷尽发明，这把长弓便是其中之一。据悉，它是静谧苔原氏族向那些北地的神祇供奉无数次祭品之后，所得到的赐礼。这把弓拥有着不可思议的力量，能重创即便是猎人团体也束手无策的巨型野兽和怪物。 | 众所周知，冰天雪地的极北之地到处都是体型超乎想象的怪物。为了在这般恶劣的环境中生存，北方民族不得不穷尽发明，这把长弓便是其中之一。据悉，它是静谧苔原氏族向那些北地的神祇供奉无数次祭品之后，所得到的赐礼。这把弓拥有着不可思议的力量，能重创即便是猎人团体也束手无策的巨型野兽和怪物。 | Correct second opening bold tag to a closing tag. |
| Traditional Chinese | 眾所周知，冰天雪地的極北之地到處都是體型超乎想象的怪物。為了在這般惡劣的環境中生存，北方民族不得不窮盡發明，這把長弓便是其中之一。據悉，它是靜謐苔原氏族向那些北地的神祇供奉無數次祭品之後，所得到的賜禮。這把弓擁有著不可思議的力量，能重創即便是獵人團體也束手無策的巨型野獸和怪物。 | 眾所周知，冰天雪地的極北之地到處都是體型超乎想象的怪物。為了在這般惡劣的環境中生存，北方民族不得不窮盡發明，這把長弓便是其中之一。據悉，它是靜謐苔原氏族向那些北地的神祇供奉無數次祭品之後，所得到的賜禮。這把弓擁有著不可思議的力量，能重創即便是獵人團體也束手無策的巨型野獸和怪物。 | Correct second opening bold tag to a closing tag. |

| English reference | — | When foes take damage from your Eldritch Blast, they must succeed a Strength Saving Throw against your Potency DC or receive Glacial Destruction until the end of the turn.<br><br>If a creature dies while under this condition, its allies in combat must succeed an easy Saving Throw or take Glacial Destruction for [1] turns. When you miss with Eldritch Blast, you become Slowed until the end of the turn (unless you have Sculpt Spells). | — |
| Brazilian Portuguese | Quando inimigos tomam dano causado por seu Eldritch Blast, eles recebem Destruição Glacial até o final do turno.<br><br>Se uma criatura morre enquanto estiver sob esse efeito, ela causa Destruição Glacial por [1] turnos em todos seus aliados em combate. Quando você errar com Eldritch Blast, você se torna Lento até o final do turno (a menos que você tenha Sculpt Spells). | Quando inimigos sofrem dano da sua Rajada Mística, devem passar em um teste de resistência de Força contra sua CD de Potência ou sofrer Destruição Glacial até o final do turno.<br><br>Se uma criatura morrer sob essa condição, seus aliados em combate devem passar em um teste de resistência fácil ou sofrer Destruição Glacial por [1] turnos. Quando você errar com a Rajada Mística, ficará Lento até o final do turno (a menos que tenha Esculpir Magias). | Restore both omitted saving throws: initial Strength save against Potency DC and easy save for spread on death. Retain the tier-specific Glacial Destruction status and miss penalty. |
| Simplified Chinese | 当敌人受到你魔能爆伤害时，直到回合结束前都会获得冰河毁灭。<br><br>如果一个生物在该状态下死亡，它会对其战斗中所有的盟友施加冰河毁灭，持续[1]回合。当你的魔能爆未命中时，直到回合结束前你都会陷入缓慢状态（除非你拥有法术塑形）。 | 敌人受到你的魔能爆伤害时，必须通过一次对抗你威能豁免等级的力量豁免检定，否则受到冰河毁灭，持续至回合结束。<br><br>若一个生物在此状态下死亡，其战斗中的盟友必须通过一次简单的豁免检定，否则受到冰河毁灭，持续[1]回合。当你的魔能爆未命中时，你陷入缓慢，持续至回合结束（除非你拥有法术塑形）。 | Restore both omitted saving-throw clauses: Strength versus Potency DC on damage, and an easy save for allies after the affected creature dies. |
| Traditional Chinese | 當敵人受到你魔能爆傷害時，直到回合結束前都會獲得冰河毀滅。<br><br>如果一個生物在該狀態下死亡，它會對其戰鬥中所有的盟友施加冰河毀滅，持續[1]回合。當你的魔能爆未命中時，直到回合結束前你都會陷入緩慢狀態（除非你擁有法術塑形）。 | 敵人受到你的魔能爆傷害時，必須通過一次對抗你威能豁免等級的力量豁免檢定，否則受到冰河毀滅，持續至回合結束。<br><br>若一個生物在此狀態下死亡，其戰鬥中的盟友必須通過一次簡單的豁免檢定，否則受到冰河毀滅，持續[1]回合。當你的魔能爆未命中時，你陷入緩慢，持續至回合結束（除非你擁有法術塑形）。 | Restore both omitted saving-throw clauses: Strength versus Potency DC on damage and an easy save for allies after the affected creature dies. |
| French | Lorsque des ennemis subissent des dégâts de votre Décharge occulte, ils reçoivent Destruction glaciale jusqu’à la fin du tour.<br><br>Si une créature meurt sous cet effet, elle inflige Destruction glaciale pendant [1] tours à tous ses alliés en combat. Si vous manquez avec Décharge occulte, vous devenez Ralenti jusqu’à la fin du tour (sauf si vous possédez Façonnage des sorts). | Lorsque des ennemis subissent des dégâts de votre Décharge occulte, ils doivent réussir un jet de sauvegarde de Force contre votre DD de Puissance ou subir Destruction glaciale jusqu’à la fin du tour.<br><br>Si une créature meurt sous cet effet, ses alliés en combat doivent réussir un jet de sauvegarde facile ou subir Destruction glaciale pendant [1] tours. Si vous manquez avec Décharge occulte, vous devenez Ralenti jusqu’à la fin du tour (sauf si vous possédez Façonnage des sorts). | Restore both omitted saving throws: initial Strength save against Potency DC and easy save for spread on death. Retain the tier-specific Glacial Destruction status and miss penalty. |
| German | Wenn Gegner durch deinen Schauriger Strahl Schaden erleiden, erhalten sie bis zum Ende des Zuges Gletscherzerstörung. <br><br>Wenn eine Kreatur unter diesem Zustand stirbt, fügt sie allen ihren Verbündeten im Kampf für [1] Runden Gletscherzerstörung zu. Wenn du mit Schauriger Strahl verfehlst, wirst du bis zum Ende des Zuges verlangsamt bis zum Ende des Zuges (es sei denn, du hast Zauber formen). | Wenn Gegner durch deinen Schaurigen Strahl Schaden erleiden, müssen sie einen Rettungswurf auf Stärke gegen deine Wirkungs-SG bestehen oder erleiden bis zum Ende des Zuges Gletscherzerstörung.<br><br>Wenn eine Kreatur unter diesem Zustand stirbt, müssen ihre Verbündeten im Kampf einen einfachen Rettungswurf bestehen oder erleiden für [1] Runden Gletscherzerstörung. Wenn du mit Schaurigem Strahl verfehlst, wirst du bis zum Ende des Zuges verlangsamt (es sei denn, du hast Zauber formen). | Restore both omitted saving throws: initial Strength save against Potency DC and easy save for spread on death. Retain the tier-specific Glacial Destruction status and miss penalty. |
| Italian | Quando i nemici subiscono danni dalla tua Deflagrazione Occulta, ricevono Distruzione Glaciale fino alla fine del turno.<br><br>Se una creatura muore mentre è sotto questa condizione, infligge Distruzione Glaciale per [1] turni a tutti i suoi alleati in combattimento. Quando manchi con la Deflagrazione Occulta, diventi Rallentato fino alla fine del turno (a meno che tu non abbia Modellare Incantesimi). | Quando i nemici subiscono danni dalla tua Deflagrazione Occulta, devono superare un tiro salvezza su Forza contro la tua CD di Potenza o subire Distruzione Glaciale fino alla fine del turno.<br><br>Se una creatura muore mentre è sotto questa condizione, i suoi alleati in combattimento devono superare un tiro salvezza facile o subire Distruzione Glaciale per [1] turni. Quando manchi con la Deflagrazione Occulta, diventi Rallentato fino alla fine del turno (a meno che tu non abbia Modellare Incantesimi). | Restore both omitted saving throws: initial Strength save against Potency DC and easy save for spread on death. Retain the tier-specific Glacial Destruction status and miss penalty. |
| Korean | 적이 당신의 섬뜩한 파동에 피해를 입으면 턴 종료 시까지 빙결 파괴 상태가 됩니다.<br><br>이 상태에서 크리처가 사망하면, 전투 중인 해당 크리처의 모든 아군에게 [1]턴 동안 빙결 파괴를 부여합니다. 섬뜩한 파동이 빗나가면 턴 종료 시까지 둔화 상태가 됩니다(주문 조정이 있는 경우 제외). | 적이 당신의 섬뜩한 파동에 피해를 입으면, 당신의 위력 내성 난이도에 대항하여 근력 내성 굴림에 성공해야 하며, 실패 시 턴 종료까지 빙결 파괴 상태가 됩니다.<br><br>이 상태에서 크리처가 사망하면, 전투 중인 해당 크리처의 아군은 쉬운 내성 굴림에 성공해야 하며, 실패 시 [1]턴 동안 빙결 파괴 상태가 됩니다. 섬뜩한 파동이 빗나가면 턴 종료까지 둔화 상태가 됩니다(주문 조정이 있는 경우 제외). | Restore both omitted saving throws: initial Strength save against Potency DC and easy save for allies when the affected creature dies. English does not guarantee either condition application. Preserve the exact Glacial Destruction tier AMP_FROST_DESTRUCTION_3 for this handle. |

## 179. Ring of Peaceful Dawn

Handle: `hd377c7abg3fdcg40d4ga3a7g21f77f4a30e8`.

| Language | Previous content | Updated content | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| English reference | — | You could ponder the origins of this ring indefinitely, debating whether it was crafted by ancient druids, powerful wizards, or monks. However, time and patience are in short supply. The primary objective is to uncover the means to activate this magnificent ring adorned with a perfectly faceted rosy spinel. You're well aware that this artifact exudes a soothing aura, but despite tens of hours of experimentation, the activation method remains elusive. Deciding that "night is the mother of counsel," you choose to don the ring as you retire for the night. To your astonishment, upon awakening, both you and your companions feel a surge of power akin to the radiant dawn sun! | — |
| Russian | Можно долго гадать о природе этого кольца: друиды это были, или один из могущественных волшебников или монахов древности, но у вас нет на это ни времени, ни терпения. Главное - это узнать, как активировать это величественное кольцо с идеально ограненным розоватым шпинелем. Вам уже известно, что артефакт источает умиротворяющую ауру, но метод активации спустя 10 часов экспериментов вам так и не дался. Подумав, что утро вечера мудренее, вы решали на всякий случай надеть колечко и легли спать. Каково же ваше удивление было, когда по подъему ваша команда ощутила мощный прилив энергии, сравнимой с силой рассветного солнца! | Можно долго гадать о природе этого кольца: друиды это были, или один из могущественных волшебников или монахов древности, но у вас нет на это ни времени, ни терпения. Главное - это узнать, как активировать это величественное кольцо с идеально ограненным розоватым шпинелем. Вам уже известно, что артефакт источает умиротворяющую ауру, но метод активации спустя десятки часов экспериментов вам так и не дался. Подумав, что утро вечера мудренее, вы решали на всякий случай надеть колечко и легли спать. Каково же ваше удивление было, когда по подъему ваша команда ощутила мощный прилив энергии, сравнимой с силой рассветного солнца! | Current English says tens of hours, not exactly 10; retain existing Russian flavour otherwise. |

## 209. The Gambler's Due

Handle: `h5bcbef87g68a5gfd62gfe59ge4a343477289`.

| Language | Previous content | Updated content | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| English reference | — | Soft gray leather, worn smooth as casino felt. A Luskan cardsharp won these elegant purple slippers in a high-stakes game against something that didn't blink once in six hours. The house always wins — except when the house is these boots. Fortune swung wild. Jackpots and busts. Survived three hits, caught two plagues. Every coin toss landed on edge. Every dice roll came up snake eyes or boxcars, never the point. Whether he died loaded or broke, grinning or screaming — the pit bosses tell different stories. | — |
| Korean | 카지노 펠트처럼 매끄럽게 닳은 부드러운 회색 가죽. 러스칸의 타짜가 6시간 동안 단 한 번도 눈을 깜빡이지 않은 무언가를 상대로 한 고액 판돈 게임에서 이 우아한 보라색 슬리퍼를 따냈습니다. 하우스는 언제나 승리합니다—하우스가 이 장화인 경우를 제외하면 말이죠. 운명은 거칠게 요동쳤습니다. 대박과 쪽박. 세 번의 습격에서 살아남았고 두 번의 전염병에 걸렸습니다. 던진 동전은 매번 옆면으로 섰습니다. 주사위 굴림은 매번 1이나 12가 나왔고, 결코 목표 숫자는 나오지 않았습니다. 그가 거액을 쥐고 죽었는지 빈털터리로 죽었는지, 웃으며 죽었는지 비명을 지르며 죽었는지는—도박장의 관리자들마다 이야기가 다릅니다. | 카지노 펠트처럼 매끄럽게 닳은 부드러운 회색 가죽. 러스칸의 타짜가 6시간 동안 단 한 번도 눈을 깜빡이지 않은 무언가를 상대로 한 고액 판돈 게임에서 이 우아한 보라색 슬리퍼를 따냈습니다. 하우스는 언제나 승리합니다—하우스가 이 장화인 경우를 제외하면 말이죠. 운명은 거칠게 요동쳤습니다. 대박과 쪽박. 세 번의 습격에서 살아남았고 두 번의 전염병에 걸렸습니다. 던진 동전은 매번 옆면으로 섰습니다. 주사위 굴림은 매번 2나 12가 나왔고, 결코 목표 숫자는 나오지 않았습니다. 그가 거액을 쥐고 죽었는지 빈털터리로 죽었는지, 웃으며 죽었는지 비명을 지르며 죽었는지는—도박장의 관리자들마다 이야기가 다릅니다. | In the numeric mismatch, English snake eyes means two dice showing one each, total 2, not 1; boxcars remains 12. |

<a id="entry-241"></a>

## 241. Whirlwind's Boots

Handle: `hee24ef03gb63fga23dg23e3gc6f321446166`.

| Language | Previous content | Updated content | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| English reference | — | Oxhide worn thin from constant footwork, sturdy straps binding too tight around boots already falling apart. These have seen their share of fighting pits, that much is plain. A slave from Thay wore them: seventy-three straight victories across Faerûn's arenas, and whispered to his fellow fighters that the boots themselves granted him power, building momentum with each exchange. First swing of the blade comes clumsy, second swifter, third already blurs to sight. By the fifth, the sixth, no opponent could match the pace. The crowd roared and roared: "Whirlwind! Whirlwind!" Pity he died in the seventy-fourth bout, by the hand of Gondo the Zhentarim, yet the boots carried Whirlwind three steps forward still before he fell. | — |
| German | Das Ochsenleder ist durch ständiges Laufen abgenutzt, die robusten Riemen sind zu fest um die bereits auseinanderfallenden Stiefel gebunden. Diese Stiefel haben schon viele Kämpfe gesehen, so viel ist klar. Ein Sklave aus Thay trug sie: 73 duge in Folge in den Arenen von Faerûn, und er flüsterte seinen Mitkämpfern zu, dass die Stiefel ihm Kraft verliehen und ihm bei jedem Schlag Schwung verliehen. Der erste Schwung der Klinge ist noch ungeschickt, der zweite schon schneller, der dritte verschwimmt bereits vor den Augen. Beim fünften, beim sechsten kann kein Gegner mehr mithalten. Die Menge brüllt und brüllt: „Whirlwind! Whirlwind!" Leider starb er im vierundsiebzigsten Kampf durch die Hand von Gondo dem Zhentarim, doch die Stiefel trugen Whirlwind noch drei Schritte vorwärts, bevor er fiel. | Das Ochsenleder ist durch ständiges Laufen abgenutzt, die robusten Riemen sind zu fest um die bereits auseinanderfallenden Stiefel gebunden. Diese Stiefel haben schon viele Kämpfe gesehen, so viel ist klar. Ein Sklave aus Thay trug sie: 73 Siege in Folge in den Arenen von Faerûn, und er flüsterte seinen Mitkämpfern zu, dass die Stiefel ihm Kraft verliehen und ihm bei jedem Schlag Schwung verliehen. Der erste Schwung der Klinge ist noch ungeschickt, der zweite schon schneller, der dritte verschwimmt bereits vor den Augen. Beim fünften, beim sechsten kann kein Gegner mehr mithalten. Die Menge brüllt und brüllt: „Whirlwind! Whirlwind!" Leider starb er im vierundsiebzigsten Kampf durch die Hand von Gondo dem Zhentarim, doch die Stiefel trugen Whirlwind noch drei Schritte vorwärts, bevor er fiel. | 73 equals seventy-three; correct corrupted noun duge to Siege (victories). |
